\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} (also further abbreviated ZRF) is the base tool for managing content with variable size on disk.

It allows a random access per logical relative rank to a user record content. Logical relative rank is the user logical rank view and does NOT correspond to physical storage order \+: \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group__recordranks}{Relative rank of a record for a ZRandom\+File or ZMaster\+File.}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}
\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} places the contents according its size to optimize its behavior, AND NOT necessarily according the record creation order.

It manages free space using specific algorithms that reallocate space whenever required and extend the file size only when required.

It shall reserve (allocate) a given disk space at creation time to avoid physical holes on disk.

\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} offers many optional features, left to the choice, in order to better manage ZRF behavior, knowing there is a choice to make between instant write/delete access time and global performance.\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGGfs}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{grab\+Free\+Space}{grabFreeSpace}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGGfs}
Grab\+Free\+Space is an on-\/flow process that consist in agglomerating free blocks before and free blocks after the current block being free. So that the deleted block grabs the available space around him. It reduces the eventuality \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item to have holes between blocks
\item to have a huge number of atomized free blocks
\end{DoxyItemize}

As grab\+Free\+Space will weight a little bit on resource consumption, it should be carefully used.

grab\+Free\+Space will offer its best when \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item using varying size record content
\item standard deviation of record size is important compared to block target size (average size of blocks existing in file-\/ or forced value)\+: \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} offers statistical tools to study the records/blocks population and to take appropriate decisions. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
block target size is a statistical value that is computed to be the average size of existing blocks withing the file.
\end{DoxyNote}
This means that \+: in case of fixed length record, grad\+Free\+Space option is not appropriate (even if it may be used without any problem), because it may induce a (small) waste of computing time.  
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGHwm}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{high\+Water\+Marking}{highWaterMarking}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGHwm}
Using high\+Water\+Marking option parameter, it will zero any removed record content. This option could be of interest to preserve some confidentiality for data.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
When using this option, surface scan will no more show up deleted block headers.
\end{DoxyNote}
\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGPerf}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Performance monitoring}{Performance monitoring}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGPerf}
\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} has multiple statistic, monitoring and dump tools that allows to have a vision on how content is stored in order to manage performance. \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_s_t_a_t_s}{Dump utilities and Statistical tools for ZRandom\+File}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Endianness \+: It is of user\textquotesingle{}s responsibility to manage the content format, and especially the numeric (arithmetic) data format in case of Endianness. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___endian_conversion}{Endianness imposes a special processing for key data.} .
\end{DoxySeeAlso}
\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGSurf}{}\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{File surface management}{File surface management}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGSurf}
There is a set of tools for
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item analyzing the file surface \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item how block records are stored
\item where are the holes and how many they are
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item reorganize the \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} in record ranks order
\item clone the file
\item extend the file
\item clear the file content
\item truncate
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGUsing}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Using ZRandom\+File}{Using ZRandomFile}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGUsing}
Despite the fact that \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} is a true relative file management system with variable content size, it allows to be used as an \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_array}{ZArray}, but with variable content. Data is handled using ZData\+Buffer data structure.

\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} offers the possibility to use standard \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_array}{ZArray} overloaded operators and methods emulated by \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File}\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item standard \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_array}{ZArray} emulation
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item push
\item push\+\_\+front
\item pop\+RP
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item operators
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item zrf\mbox{[}id\mbox{]} \+: returns the record of rank id
\item zrf \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} ZData\+Buffer \+: equivalent to push
\end{DoxyItemize}

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
move \+: Method not allowed -\/ only low level move may be made between two blocks (protected method) swap \+: Method not allowed
\end{DoxyNote}
The difference with \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_array}{ZArray} is that all these operators return a reference read-\/only data. This means that data cannot be modified in the file using these methods.
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGMainMeth}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Main methods}{Main methods}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGMainMeth}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item zcreate \+: creates the file and its infra-\/data structures. \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_random_file_group_ga288c5dddc2f08d3cbed9737f5830bb49}{ZRandom\+File\+::zcreate}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\item zopen \+: opens the file.
\item zadd \+: adds a record logically positionned at the end of the record list. Physically the block containing the user\textquotesingle{}s record may be anywhere on the file\textquotesingle{}s surface according the better placement capability.
\item zinsert \+: insert a record at a given rank
\item zremove \+: logically deletes a record. block containing record is then placed in free block pool. if grab\+Free\+Space option is chosen then adjacent free blocks are agglomerated to make one unique free block and benefit from the whole space set.
\item zget \+: reads a record from file according to its logical relative position and returns records content.
\item zgetnext \+: reads the next record
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGSpecial}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Special methods}{Special methods}}\label{group___z_random_file_group_ZRFGSpecial}
There is a set of other methods for \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Content dump and statistics \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_s_t_a_t_s}{Dump utilities and Statistical tools for ZRandom\+File}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\item File surface utilities management \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_utilities}{ZRandom\+File Surface management utilities}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\item Fields access
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Free search using fieldsrecord \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_free_search}{Searching in free sequential mode}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\item content change using field definition \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_field_access}{Accessing a ZRandom\+File by Field (\+Segment of record)}
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\end{DoxyItemize}
\item zrecover \+: recovers a free pool block. It creates back a block from first freed block contained in free block and ungrab all deleted block agglomerated to it. these undeleted blocks become free blocks in free pool and can be themselves recovered.
\end{DoxyItemize}

~\newline

ZRandom\+File\+::\+\_\+add2\+Phases\+Commit\+\_\+\+Commit Physically writes the record (and block) content to content file using information given from add\+\_\+\+Prepare. 

creates the block containing user record (p\+User\+Buffer). Block is then marked in ZBAT pool as ZBS\+\_\+\+Used. Writes everything on file Updates file control block on file.

ZRandom\+File\+::\+\_\+get returns a block record corresponding to its rank given by p\+Rank. Eventually locks the block read using p\+Lock lock mask. 

Returns a ZStatus \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item ZS\+\_\+\+SUCCESS if everything went well
\item ZS\+\_\+\+BOUNDHIGH if p\+Rank is outside (high) the active block pool index set. (corresponds to EOF)
\item ZS-\/\+BOUNDLOW if p\+Rank is outside (low) the active block pool index set
\end{DoxyItemize}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZStatus}} ZRandom\+File\+::\+\_\+rename\+Bck (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{Zai\+Errors \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{p\+Error\+Log = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{nullptr}}},  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{bool}}}]{p\+No\+Except = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{false}}},  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{const}} \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{char}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{p\+Bck\+Ext = {\ttfamily "{}bck"{}} }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



ZStandard\+Backup\+File\+Names ZRandom file suite has a standard notation for backuping files and keeping in line their notations. $<$base filename$>$.$<$extension$>$\+\_\+$<$bckext$>$$<$nn$>$ where $<$base filename$>$ is a base file name composed of $<$root file name$>$.$<$file extension$>$ $<$bckext$>$ is a suffix chosen by application $<$nn$>$ is a digit number giving backup \textquotesingle{}version\textquotesingle{}. 

IMPORTANT\+: ZRandom file suite insure that $<$nn$>$ is the same for all files concerned by backup operation.

\doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File}\+:\+:{\itshape rename\+Bck local method that renames \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} structure (content and header files) whose content is p\+Content\+Path renaming with a special extension suffix given by p\+Bck\+Ext plus a incremental 2 digit value (nn) as follows \+: $<$base filename$>$.$<$extension$>$}$<$p\+Bck\+Ext$>$$<$nn$>$

p\+Bck\+Ext is defaulted to string "{}bck"{}.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
a ZStatus. In case of error, ZStatus is returned and ZException is set with appropriate message.\+see\+: \doxylink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZBSError} @errors \+: ZS\+\_\+\+INVOP \+: file is already open when trying to open it ZS\+\_\+\+FILENOTEXIST \+: file does not exist while it must exist for requested open mode ZS\+\_\+\+ERROPEN \+: low level file I/O error \+: errno is captured and system message is provided. ZS\+\_\+\+LOCKED \+: file is locked ZS\+\_\+\+BADFILETYPE \+: file type is not what expected. ZS\+\_\+\+MODEINVALID \+: open mode is incompatible with requested operation or open mode is not allowed by file lock.
\end{DoxyReturn}
ZS\+\_\+\+FILEERROR \+: cannot physically remove file (either content or header) ZException is loaded with errno and appropriate error explainations. 
\end{DoxyItemize}

\+\_\+rename\+To renames current Randomfile from its current name to p\+New\+Content\+Name, renames its header file accordingly. if file is open then file will be closed and will remain closed when exiting routine. if p\+Error\+Log is nullptr, then no message will be issued -\/ however ZException will be positioned in case of error.

a ZStatus

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{generateURIHeader()}{generateURIHeader()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZStatus}} generate\+URIHeader (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{uri\+String}]{p\+URIPath,  }\item[{uri\+String \&}]{p\+URIHeader }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



generate\+URIHeader Generates the appropriate ZRandom\+File Header file name from the content file path name (user name) 

Preprocessor parameter {\bfseries{HEADER\+\_\+\+FILEEXTENSION}} gives the extension name of all header files. This parameter is set to \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}.zrh\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} by default.

This parameter is defined in file \doxylink{zrandomfiletypes_8h}{zrandomfiletypes.\+h}

\begin{DoxyWarning}{Warning}
It is then forbidden to give a ZRandom\+File such an extension. If so, an error will occur when trying to create the ZRF file, ZS\+\_\+\+INVNAME will be returned and ZException will be setup appropriately. In other cases, ZS\+\_\+\+SUCCES will be returned 
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+URIPath} & uri\+String containing the user named ZRandom\+File path (Input) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ out}}  & {\em p\+URIHeader} & uri\+String containing the generated header file name (Output) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
a ZStatus. In case of error, ZStatus is returned and ZException is set with appropriate message.\+see\+: \doxylink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZBSError} 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\doxylink{group___z_random_file_group_ga288c5dddc2f08d3cbed9737f5830bb49}{ZRandom\+File\+::zcreate}. 

File creation with only 1 value \+: Initial size . Other parameters are taken as their default value.

File path must have been set previously with \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file_a097cbb67f4928d593dbb852f7b417ba0}{ZRandom\+File\+::set\+Path()}.

Initial size represents the amount of space initially allocated to the file at creation time.

This space will be placed in free blocks pool (ZFree\+Block\+Pool) and will be immediately available for use to be allocated as data blocks.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Initial\+Size} & Initial file space in byte that is allocated at creation time. This space is placed in Free block pool as one block. \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{ in}}  & {\em p\+Leave\+Open} & if set to true file is left open after its creation with open mode mask (ZRF\+\_\+\+Exclusive \texorpdfstring{$\vert$}{|} ZRF\+\_\+\+All ). If false, file is closed. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
a ZStatus. In case of error, ZStatus is returned and ZException is set with appropriate message.\+see\+: \doxylink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{ZBSError} 
\end{DoxyReturn}
